### [22] [The Evaluation Context Protocol (ECP): A Portable Contract for AI Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19263)
*Aniket Wattamwar,Manav Anandani,Mrunal Kakirwar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究从评估单一大语言模型转向评估自主智能体架构的方法，并提出处于早期阶段的、供应商中立的评估上下文协议（ECP），以统一智能体输出、工具调用和审计信息的评估接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估存在基准利用、过度自信但错误、理论能力与实际运行可靠性不一致，以及评估基础设施碎片化等问题。研究旨在建立跨框架、可集成持续集成系统的标准化评估契约层。

Method: 论文分析智能体的架构组成与现有评估范式的局限，设计基于JSON-RPC的ECP接口，使智能体暴露用户可见输出、工具调用记录和评估安全的审计上下文，并支持统一执行程序化检查。同时开发开源参考实现及面向LangChain、LlamaIndex、CrewAI和PydanticAI的适配器。

Result: 论文提出了ECP的初步协议设计和参考实现，说明其可作为不同智能体框架与持续集成系统之间的可移植评估层，并将设计与近期文献记录的失败模式进行对照。但当前尚未提供充分的实证验证，协议评估面、方法集合和评分器类型仍可能调整。

Conclusion: ECP为智能体评估标准化提供了一个具有实践价值的早期基础，尤其适合统一观测与程序化检查；不过它仍是进行中的工作，而非成熟标准。其有效性、通用性和采用价值需要通过更多系统上的实验与经验验证来确认。

Abstract: The evolution of artificial intelligence has necessitated a fundamental shift from evaluating isolated Large Language Models (LLMs) to assessing autonomous agentic architectures. This paper explores the critical methodologies for evaluating AI agents and the essential role of advanced observability infrastructure. We analyze the architectural components of agents and identify the severe limitations of current evaluation paradigms, including benchmark exploitation, the "confidently wrong" phenomenon, and the discrepancy between theoretical capability and operational reliability. To begin addressing the fragmentation in current evaluation infrastructure, this paper proposes the Evaluation Context Protocol (ECP), an early-stage, vendor-neutral framework intended to act as a portable evaluation contract layer for agentic systems. In its current form ECP defines a small JSON-RPC interface over which an agent exposes its user-visible output, the tool calls it made, and evaluator-safe audit context, and against which programmatic checks can be run uniformly across frameworks and continuous integration systems. We describe an open-source reference implementation that includes adapters for LangChain, LlamaIndex, CrewAI, and PydanticAI, and we situate the design against failure modes documented in the recent literature. ECP is presented as work in progress rather than a finished standard: the evaluation surface, method set, and grader families are all expected to change as the protocol is exercised against more systems, and the empirical validation required to justify adoption is outlined as future work.

</details>


### [23] [Stable Within, Unidentified Across: Semantic Identification of Benchmark Effects and Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19269)
*Xi Qin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究表明，评估结论取决于评估者控制的语义规范。TraceElephant中，不同语义族对采用效果和排序给出不一致结论；联合审计族无法识别唯一的真实效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在检验法律引用、可评分性和聚合方式等评估语义变化是否会改变结论，并区分某一固定规范下的稳定性与跨规范的可识别性。

Method: 定义当端点Q(s)在声明的规范族内保持不变时为评估语义可识别。基于冻结的217行分析，比较非对称族F_asym、对比可比且联合冻结的F_cc，以及二者并集F_audit；进一步采用析因审计分解分歧来源。

Result: F_asym得到任务不重叠采用效果约为−40.76至−43.03个百分点；F_cc中的两种对称处理均得到0，区间为[0,0]。因此F_cc可识别T=0，但F_audit无法识别T。析因审计显示100%的分歧来自单侧终端删除，非终端因素贡献为0；排序仍依赖具体规范。

Conclusion: 固定规范族内部的稳定结果并不意味着跨规范的结论可识别。该研究提供了一个局部的非蕴含见证和可复用的、按规范族索引的审计框架，但不是普遍率估计、穷尽性的语义分类或独立复现。

Abstract: Evaluation conclusions depend on evaluator-controlled semantics: legal references, scoreability, and aggregation. We call an artifact-defined endpoint Q(s) evaluation-semantically identified when it is invariant over a declared family. A frozen 217-row analysis appears stable within its restricted contract family. In TraceElephant, F_asym yields precise task-disjoint adoption effects from -40.76 to -43.03 percentage points, whereas both jointly frozen contrast-comparable treatments in F_cc yield exactly zero with [0, 0] intervals. Their union F_audit therefore does not identify T, even though F_cc identifies T = 0. A factorial audit attributes 100 percent of the disagreement to one-sided terminal deletion, with zero nonterminal contribution. Rankings remain specification-dependent across the symmetric treatments. This is a localized non-implication witness and reusable family-indexed audit, not a prevalence estimate, exhaustive semantic taxonomy, or independent replication.

</details>


### [24] [Hype Meets Reality: Large Language Models as Mutators in Search-based Automated Program Repair of Simulink-Stateflow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19347)
*Ayesha Irshad,Pablo Valle,Jon Ayerdi,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究用大语言模型生成的变异操作替代部分传统变异操作，以改进面向Simulink/Stateflow模型的搜索式自动程序修复；受控实验表明，该朴素集成方式显著降低了修复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统搜索式自动程序修复依赖预先设计的变异操作，灵活性和表达能力有限。大语言模型具备生成式修复能力，因此本文探讨其能否动态生成更丰富的候选补丁，并应用于信息物理系统模型修复。

Method: 在FlowRepair基础上，将部分变异操作替换为大语言模型生成的变异操作；使用涵盖四个信息物理系统领域的19个真实故障Stateflow模型，并在相同实验设置和墙钟时间预算下，与原始FlowRepair进行受控比较。

Result: 不同大语言模型版本生成了适用于4至6个模型的合理补丁，并为4个模型生成有效补丁；原始FlowRepair分别达到18个和16个模型。分析显示，大语言模型难以执行精确的符号编辑，缺乏行为反馈，并引入噪声较大的搜索空间，从而削弱搜索效率。

Conclusion: 在当前集成方式和实验设置下，大语言模型不能直接有效替代结构化变异操作。结果并不意味着大语言模型普遍不适用于自动程序修复，而是表明朴素集成存在明显局限；更可行的方向是结合结构化变异与生成式引导的混合式方法。

Abstract: Search-based Automated Program Repair (APR) techniques rely on carefully designed mutation operators to explore the space of candidate fixes. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) suggest that generative models could replace such operators by dynamically proposing repairs. In this paper, we investigate this hypothesis in the context of Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs) modeled in Simulink/Stateflow. We extend the state-of-the-art FlowRepair approach by replacing a subset of its mutation operators with LLM-generated mutations, enabling more flexible and expressive patch generation. We evaluate the approach on a benchmark of 19 real-world faulty Stateflow models across four CPS domains, using the same experimental setup as FlowRepair for controlled comparison under the same wall-clock budget. Contrary to expectations, in this controlled evaluation, the LLM-based mutation substantially degrades repair performance under the FlowRepair experimental setup. Across the tested LLM variants, the LLM-based repair produced plausible patches for 4-6 models and valid patches for 4 models, compared to 18 and 16, respectively, with the original approach. Our analysis reveals that, in this integration, LLMs struggle with precise symbolic edits, lack behavioral feedback, and generate a noisy search space that hinders effective exploration. Rather than showing a general limitation of LLMs for APR, these findings highlight fundamental limitations of naively integrating LLMs into search-based APR and motivate hybrid approaches that combine structured mutation with generative guidance.

</details>


### [25] [RD-Gen: Random DAG Generator Considering Multi-rate Applications for Reproducible Scheduling Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19460)
*Atsushi Yano,Takuya Azumi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RD-Gen，一种面向多速率应用、支持批量生成随机DAG任务集的工具，用于提升实时调度评估的可复现性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时系统日益复杂，DAG模型被广泛用于描述任务依赖和并行性。现有随机DAG生成工具无法充分考虑多速率应用，研究者需自行构造数据集，增加工作量并削弱实验的可靠性与可复现性。

Method: 设计并实现RD-Gen随机DAG生成器，纳入多速率应用特征，并支持按照不同参数批量生成随机DAG任务集；通过案例研究验证其对多种问题设置和DAG研究需求的适应能力。

Result: 案例研究表明，RD-Gen能够生成适用于不同参数配置和研究场景的随机DAG任务集，并支持多速率应用下的调度评估。

Conclusion: RD-Gen为实时系统中的随机DAG生成提供了统一、灵活且可复现的工具，有助于降低实验数据构造成本并提升调度算法评估的可靠性。

Abstract: Real-time systems have various requirements such as the deadline and resource constraints. In addition, real-time systems are becoming larger and more complex, and studies on performance analysis and efficient scheduling algorithms are becoming increasingly important. Directed acyclic graph (DAG) models, which can express task dependencies and parallelism, are used for such studies. Random DAG sets are used to demonstrate the effectiveness and objectivity of methods proposed for real-time systems. However, there is no random DAG generation tool available that can generate a DAG set that considers the latest multi-rate applications. Therefore, researchers need to generate random DAG sets on their own, leading to additional effort and reduced reliability and reproducibility. To solve this problem, we propose a random DAG generator considering multi-rate applications for reproducible scheduling evaluation (RD-Gen). RD-Gen also enables batch generation of random DAG sets with different parameters. Case studies are used to demonstrate that RD-Gen can manage various problem settings and DAG study requirements.

</details>


### [26] [Measuring What a Specification Determines: A Formal Semantic-Block Model and an Execution-Judged Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19475)
*Oleg Grynets,Dmytro Kostetskyi,Vasyl Lyashkevych*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“语义块”形式化规格模型及由执行结果裁决的评测基准，用于将规格质量与模型能力区分开来评估。实证表明，该模型在上下文压缩、覆盖率和结构恢复性方面表现良好；但对所评测的当代大语言模型而言，“确定性”虽具形式理论意义，尚不能单独作为稳定的经验质量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统规格评估常混合了规格本身的清晰度与实现者或模型的能力，难以判断失败究竟源于规格歧义还是实现能力不足。论文旨在建立可机器检查、可执行验证的规格表示与基准，从而更严格地量化规格对独立实现结果一致性的影响。

Method: 作者将规格表示为包含语义块、依赖关系、块所属规则、决策点和显式开放问题的结构，并定义四项良构条件：无环性、单一归属、约束支配以及全覆盖或歧义停止。确定性在理论上定义为所有符合规格的实现之间达成一致，并以独立实现者的结果收敛性进行经验估计。模型应用于一个Oracle至PostgreSQL迁移规格，含18个语义块和19条依赖边；基准固定实现者面板，设置强制无规格对照组，并以PostgreSQL 16及实时Oracle实例执行裁决。研究还包含三项预注册操纵实验和三项诊断分析。

Result: 五层分解通过依赖闭包将单任务平均上下文降低约71%；覆盖研究定义的Oracle构造分类体系中85.5%的项目，剩余缺口均被分诊处理；在测试的替代划分中未被帕累托支配；基于未参与原始结构定义的引用推导边，结构恢复结果达到99.9百分位。25个单元的重复运行显示显著经验波动下限：不同实验臂效果差异的中位数跨度为14.4个百分点。这说明执行评测虽可提供客观裁决，但实现结果仍受较大随机性或模型行为变异影响。

Conclusion: 语义块模型提供了可验证、可分解且具较强工程实用性的规格组织框架，执行裁决基准也有助于隔离规格效应。论文最重要的限制性结论是：确定性可作为严格的形式语义性质，但在当前大语言模型实现环境中，受重复运行变异影响，不能脱离不确定性分析而被用作单一经验规格质量指标。

Abstract: This work introduces a formal semantic-block model for specifications and an execution-judged benchmark for evaluating specification quality independently of model capability. A specification is represented as a structure comprising semantic blocks, dependency relations, block-owned rules, decision points, and explicitly open questions, subject to four machine-checkable well-formedness conditions: acyclicity, single ownership, constraint domination, and totality or ambiguity-stop. Determinacy is defined model-theoretically as agreement among all conforming implementations and is estimated empirically through convergence across independent implementers. The model is instantiated on an Oracle-to-PostgreSQL migration specification containing 18 blocks and 19 dependency edges. Computational validation shows that the five-layer decomposition reduces mean per-task context by approximately 71% through dependency closures, covers 85.5% of the study-defined Oracle construct taxonomy with all identified gaps triaged, is not Pareto-dominated by the tested alternative partitions, and is recovered at the 99.9th percentile from citation-derived edges not used to define the original structure. The benchmark keeps the implementer panel fixed, includes a mandatory no-specification control arm, and uses PostgreSQL 16 and a live Oracle instance as deterministic execution judges. Six designed studies, including three pre-registered manipulations and three diagnostic analyses, further examine specification effects. Repeated runs on a 25-unit subsample reveal an empirical variability floor with a median arm-delta spread of 14.4 percentage points. The results support determinacy as a formal concept but not as a standalone empirical quality metric for the evaluated contemporary LLM implementers.

</details>


### [27] [Accelerated Genetic Programming Hyper-Heuristics for Simulation-Based Scheduling via Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19487)
*Heyang Thomas Li,Alexander Pletzer,Yuan Tian,Yi Mei,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出以Claude智能体辅助重构Python离散事件项目调度仿真代码的方法，在HPC环境中以基准测试和正确性校验为约束，实现运行时间从1,298秒降至不足200秒，且输出保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学计算中的定制Python代码会随实验规模扩大而出现严重性能瓶颈。离散事件仿真依赖顺序状态更新、嵌套循环、条件判断及面向对象结构，难以直接受益于常见的编译数值库或GPU加速；而人工性能优化又需要较强的软件工程能力和较多迭代时间。

Method: 作者采用人机协同的系统化重构流程：以真实项目调度工作负载为对象，在高性能计算环境中设定代表性基准与输出正确性检查；由Claude智能体定位性能热点、实施针对性代码优化并评估效果；研究者保留审查、验证和最终决策权。

Result: 优化后测试运行时间由1,298秒减少至200秒以下，性能提升超过6倍，且计算输出未改变。按文中估算，该改进每年可节省约400万核小时及32万新西兰元的计算成本。

Conclusion: 智能体辅助重构可成为优化科研Python代码的实用途径，尤其适用于传统数组化和GPU方案效果有限的状态驱动型离散事件仿真。其关键前提是以可重复基准和严格正确性验证约束智能体行为，并由研究者承担最终质量控制。

Abstract: Python is widely used in scientific research because it enables rapid development and provides rich ecosystems for data analysis, artificial intelligence (AI), and machine learning. However, customized research code can become prohibitively slow as experiments scale. This challenge is particularly acute in discrete-event project-scheduling simulations, where sequential state updates, nested loops, conditional evaluations, and object-oriented structures limit the benefits of compiled numerical and GPU-accelerated libraries. Addressing these bottlenecks typically requires iterative profiling, refactoring, testing, and validation, yet researchers may lack the time or specialized software-engineering expertise for low-level optimization. This paper presents a systematic refactoring approach using Claude agentic AI on real-world project-scheduling workloads in a high-performance computing (HPC) environment. Guided by representative benchmarks and correctness checks, the agent identifies bottlenecks, implements targeted optimizations, and evaluates their effects, while the researcher retains final control. Testing runtime reduced from 1,298 seconds to under 200 seconds without changing outputs, saving four million core-hours (NZ\$320,000) annually.

</details>


### [28] [An Agentic RAG and Evaluation Framework for Assurance Case Generation: Industrial Use Case for the EU Cyber Resilience Act Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19509)
*Fariz Ikhwantri,Iker Lasa Ojanguren,Dusica Marijan,Maria I. Maslioukova,José Arias Marin,Pavlos Kosmides*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于智能体检索增强生成（Agentic RAG）的自动化框架，用于依据形式化的主张—论证—证据逻辑生成符合欧盟《网络韧性法案》（CRA）要求的保证论证，并在物联网监测系统案例中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业面临网络安全合规评估复杂、成本高和人工证据整理负担重等问题。研究旨在自动映射技术文档与CRA要求，提升合规证据生成的效率、可追溯性和可解释性。

Method: 框架采用智能体检索增强生成技术，从技术文档中检索并组织相关证据，按照形式化的Claim-Argument-Evidence结构生成保证论证（ACs）。研究结合案例验证，并使用自然语言推理（NLI）评估器衡量证据支撑与一致性，同时通过专家评价检验论证的合理性。

Result: 在Catalink的PATROLIoT监测系统案例中，框架生成了70个保证论证，平均每个论证包含约4.4个支撑工件，显示出较高的证据关联密度。NLI评估器准确率达到0.88，专家评价的合理性得分为3.06，表明生成结果具备较好的证据 grounding、可追溯性和可解释性。

Conclusion: 该方法为中小企业自动化执行CRA合规评估提供了可扩展且可解释的技术路径，能够减少人工工作量，并在保持透明决策支持的同时增强合规证据管理能力。

Abstract: Complying with the EU Cyber Resilience Act (CRA) is a resource-intensive challenge for SMEs due to the complexity of cybersecurity conformity assessment. Yet, it is essential for demonstrating regulatory compliance and ensuring product security and resilience. To address this, we introduce an automated framework for generating Assurance Cases (ACs) using an agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation grounded in a formal Claim-Argument-Evidence logic. By systematically mapping technical documentation requirements, the framework streamlines the generation of certification evidence. We validate our approach on a case study of Catalink's PATROLIoT wildfire monitoring system, where the agentic RAG generated 70 ACs with high grounding density (~4.4 artefacts per AC). The proposed Natural Language Inference (NLI) evaluator achieves 0.88 accuracy, which provides robust evidence grounding and traceability, while expert-validated plausibility (3.06) supports interpretable justifications. For practitioners, this work provides a scalable, interpretable approach for automating mandatory CRA conformity assessments, reducing manual effort while maintaining transparent decision support.

</details>


### [29] [Trace-Based Execution-Level Observability of VDM-SL Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19510)
*Tomohiro Oda,Han-Myung Chang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出记录并利用VDM解释执行过程中的赋值、操作调用与返回事件，将瞬时运行行为持久化为可分析的、基于状态的执行模型，并介绍其事件数据模型、ViennaTalk实现及可视化应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VDM动画和调试器可支持规格验证及逐步观察，但运行时内部行为通常只在执行期间可见，难以复查、系统分析或以模型形式呈现。论文旨在将这些行为保存为执行轨迹，以增强规格调试、理解和验证能力。

Method: 作者定义执行轨迹中的事件数据模型，覆盖赋值、操作调用和返回等关键执行事件；在ViennaTalk中实现轨迹记录与利用机制；再将所记录的事件序列转换或映射为状态基础模型，用于后续分析和可视化。

Result: 该方案使操作内部的执行过程能够被持久记录，并以状态演化形式进行分析和展示。论文的主要产出包括执行事件的数据模型、ViennaTalk中的实现，以及面向轨迹的可视化应用。摘要未提供定量实验指标或与其他工具的性能比较。

Conclusion: 通过把解释执行中的关键事件转化为可保存、可分析的轨迹，本文扩展了VDM动画和调试的能力，使用户不仅能实时观察执行，还能在执行后基于状态模型理解和检查操作行为。

Abstract: VDM has been pursuing rigorous verification through mathematical theorem proving and software testing via simulated execution. Animation through an interpreter enables validation of the specification to ensure it meets the required functionality. Step-by-step execution in a debugger also allows the user to follow the internal behavior of operations. In this paper, we propose the recording and utilization of execution traces of assignments, operation calls, and return statements to make the internal behavior of operations persistent and analyzable as state-based models. The data model of events in execution traces, its implementation in ViennaTalk, and its application to visualization will be introduced.

</details>


### [30] [Garbage Collection and Energy Consumption in Java: A Controlled Study Across Workloads and JDKs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19520)
*Rahil Sharma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究表明，在所评估的Java应用、负载强度与JDK组合中，Parallel的平均能耗最低，但垃圾收集器类型的总体差异未达到统计显著；负载强度才是更稳定、更显著的能耗影响因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垃圾收集器是Java运行时易于调整的配置项，可能影响执行性能和处理器能耗。该研究旨在检验不同收集器的总体能耗排序能否跨应用、负载和JDK发行版稳定泛化。

Method: 采用受控实验，比较Serial、Parallel和G1三类收集器；覆盖3个Java应用、3种工作负载强度及OpenJDK和Oracle JDK两种发行版。通过EnergiBridge测量处理器封装能耗与执行时间，并计算补充性的能耗—性能指标；使用随机区组设计方差分析检验效应，并分析能耗与执行时间的相关性。

Result: Parallel的平均能耗为839.8 J，Serial为857.6 J，G1为969.0 J，但方差分析未确认收集器类型具有统计可靠的总体能耗效应。重负载的能耗显著高于轻、中负载。能耗与执行时间呈中等正相关（r=0.33），但并非近似线性或成比例关系。OpenJDK与Oracle JDK之间未发现显著能耗差异。

Conclusion: 不存在可普遍认定为能耗最优的Java垃圾收集器。管理能耗时，应优先关注工作负载强度；垃圾收集器选择应基于目标应用、具体运行环境和实测结果进行调优，而不应仅依赖跨场景的平均排名。

Abstract: Garbage-collector selection is a low-effort configuration decision that can influence both the performance and energy consumption of Java applications. However, it remains unclear whether aggregate energy rankings among collectors generalise across heterogeneous applications, workload intensities, and JDK distributions. This study presents a controlled empirical evaluation of Serial, Parallel, and G1 garbage collection across three Java applications, three workload intensities, and two JDK distributions: OpenJDK and Oracle JDK. Using EnergiBridge, processor-package energy consumption and execution time were measured and complementary energy-performance metrics derived. Across all evaluated configurations, Parallel recorded the lowest mean energy consumption (839.8 J), followed closely by Serial (857.6 J) and G1 (969.0 J), but an RCBD ANOVA did not establish a statistically reliable collector effect. Workload intensity, by contrast, was a significant driver of energy consumption, with heavy workloads consuming substantially more energy than light or medium workloads regardless of collector. Energy consumption showed a moderate positive association with execution time (r = 0.33), indicating that longer-running configurations tended toward higher energy use, though the relationship was far from proportional. No statistically significant energy difference was found between OpenJDK and Oracle JDK. Overall, the results do not support a universally energy-optimal garbage collector; instead, workload intensity emerges as the more reliable lever for managing Java energy consumption, and collector selection should be treated as an application-specific tuning decision supported by measurement on the target system rather than aggregate rankings alone.

</details>


### [31] [Auditing and Decomposing Feedback-Driven Evolution in LLM Test Generation under the Oracle Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19626)
*Yunhao Liang,Chengguang Gan,Ruixuan Ying,Hanjun Wei,Zhe Cui,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文指出，基于执行反馈的LLM测试生成评测易受“单一参考实现充当真值”影响：无效或规格不足的输入会造成伪故障与虚假的进化收益。经多实现审计和安慰剂对照后，突变式进化的优势大幅减弱，独立重采样反而更优，细粒度反馈的实际贡献缺乏稳健证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反馈驱动测试生成通常将候选程序的执行输出直接视为可靠监督信号。然而，当测试输入本身不合法、未被问题规格定义或存在歧义时，单个已接受程序的行为不能代表语义真值，可能夸大测试生成方法发现缺陷和迭代优化的能力。论文旨在量化这一评测偏差，并区分验证器伪影、迭代流程本身的作用与真实反馈收益。

Method: 作者在142个开发任务、114个锁定外部任务和138个留出任务上评估两种代码模型、三个随机种子，并使用与被测对象交叉匹配的真实提交。首先以三个已接受实现的一致输出构成面板参照，审计生成输入及其输出正确性；其次比较单参考预言机与审计后预言机下的进化收益，并在相同预算下比较独立重采样和基于突变的进化；最后，将真实三轮反馈循环与密度匹配的安慰剂循环比较。两名软件工程博士生还对面板否定的输入进行了盲法语义审计。

Result: 在三个已接受实现一致的外部输入上，生成输出与面板一致的比例仅为27.79%和50.12%。单参考预言机将进化带来的测量增益夸大9.46至14.85个百分点；审计后，等预算独立重采样比突变进化高6.01至18.83个百分点。真实反馈相对安慰剂的外部Real-Placebo差异为+0.13和-0.50个百分点，留出集差异为+1.99和+0.28个百分点，均不足以稳健支持细粒度反馈收益。盲法审计显示，面板否定输入中94.41%无效，3.60%有效，说明多实现不一致具有诊断价值但不能直接等同于语义判定。

Conclusion: 执行反馈不能默认视为自验证信号。评估自进化测试生成器时，应采用审计与安慰剂协议：用多实现一致性和语义审查识别预言机伪影，设置预算匹配的独立重采样基线，使用安慰剂循环剥离交互脚手架效应，并仅将经可靠验证支持的性能差异归因于反馈机制。

Abstract: Execution feedback is often treated as a self-verifying signal for improving LLM-generated tests. However, when generated inputs are executed on a single accepted program and its outputs are used as ground truth, invalid or underspecified inputs can create spurious fault detections and apparent evolutionary gains. We audit this failure mode in feedback-driven test generation using 142 development tasks, 114 locked external tasks, and 138 held-out tasks, with two code models, three seeds, and fault-cross-fitted real submissions. On external inputs for which three accepted implementations agree, generated outputs match the panel on only 27.79% and 50.12% of cases. A single-reference oracle inflates the measured gain from evolution by 9.46-14.85 percentage points; after auditing, equal-budget independent resampling outperforms mutation-based evolution by 6.01-18.83 points. We further compare a genuine three-round feedback loop with a density-matched placebo. External Real-Placebo differences are +0.13 and -0.50 points, while held-out differences are +1.99 and +0.28 points and do not provide robust evidence of fine-grained feedback benefit. A blinded semantic audit by two software engineering doctoral students classifies 94.41% of panel-disconfirmed inputs as invalid but 3.60% as valid, showing that panel disagreement is informative but not semantic proof. We propose an audit-and-placebo protocol that separates verifier artifacts, interaction scaffolding, and grounded feedback credit in evaluations of self-evolving test generators.

</details>


### [32] [Loreley: Repository-Scale Program Evolution with Quality-Diversity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19703)
*Mohan Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文评估了Loreley这一基于质量-多样性（QD）档案的代码代理搜索框架。其核心机制确实保留并复用了非主线候选状态，但在受控的Zstandard实验中，48个任务预算下未证明其终点性能优于顺序冠军编辑或独立根节点提案；顺序冠军的观测均值和中位数最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序式代理搜索可持续积累当前最优候选的修改，却会丢弃替代分支；独立提案保留探索广度，但每次都从初始状态重启。研究旨在检验：保存完整仓库状态的QD档案，并将档案状态作为后续编辑的父本或上下文，能否通过“垫脚石”路径带来更高质量的代码优化结果。

Method: Loreley将候选表示为隔离worktree中生成的Git提交，由项目提供的评估器打分；QD档案保存完整仓库状态，并用于父本采样或上下文供给。实验在Zstandard任务上比较配置后的Loreley QD、顺序冠军编辑和独立根节点提案：共7个配对区组，每种策略每区组48个实际候选任务，总计1,008个任务；均从根状态初始化，但保持各策略原生并发度。每个预算检查点通过验证集选择候选，再以对代理隐藏的留出集评估固定候选。另对QD轨迹进行回溯祖先分析，以检查非现任状态和上下文“灵感边”的参与情况。

Result: 在48个任务时，QD相对顺序冠军低0.135%，配对效应的95% BCa区间为-0.556%至+0.161%；QD相对独立根节点高0.320%，区间为-0.082%至+0.686%。两组区间均未确立QD优势，且顺序冠军具有最高的48任务观测均值和中位数。QD档案确实发生了状态保留和后续采样：7个最终QD优胜者中，4个的主要父系祖先包含按回溯“单现任”规则认定的非现任状态；纳入灵感边后为6个，但这不能证明所供上下文直接导致了相应编辑。此前3次能力验证中，Loreley曾在两个Python库及另一项Zstandard修订中产生第4代、多文件改进。

Conclusion: Loreley验证了QD档案能够在代码代理搜索中保存和调动替代分支，并出现符合垫脚石机制的轨迹证据；但在当前匹配实验、48任务预算和终点评估指标下，没有统计上可靠的性能优势。结论应限定为机制被激活而终点收益未获证实，而非QD方法无效；后续应考察更大预算、不同任务类型、档案采样策略及对上下文因果作用的更直接测量。

Abstract: Sequential agent search accumulates changes from its current champion but discards alternative branches; independent proposals preserve breadth but restart from the root. Loreley instead retains complete repository states in a Quality-Diversity (QD) archive and samples them as parents or supplies them as context for later edits. Candidates are Git commits produced in isolated worktrees and judged by a project-supplied evaluator. We compare configured Loreley QD, sequential champion editing, and independent root proposals in a matched Zstandard experiment: seven paired blocks and 48 physical candidate jobs per policy and block (1,008 total), with root-only initialization and each policy's native concurrency. Validation selected a winner at each budget checkpoint; an agent-hidden holdout measured the fixed candidate. At 48 jobs, QD was 0.135% below Sequential Champion (95% BCa interval for the paired effect: -0.556% to +0.161%) and 0.320% above Independent Root (-0.082% to +0.686%). Neither contrast established a QD advantage; Sequential had the highest observed 48-job mean and median. Archive retention and later sampling did occur. Four of seven final QD winners had a non-incumbent state in their primary-parent ancestry under a retrospective one-incumbent rule applied only to the observed QD stream. Including inspiration edges raised the count to six, without showing that supplied context caused an edit. Three earlier capability campaigns produced generation-4, multi-file improvements in two Python libraries and a separate Zstandard revision. Loreley engaged the intended stepping-stone mechanism, but the controlled experiment did not show an endpoint benefit at 48 jobs.

</details>


### [33] [A Fully Automated, Deployment-Aware Testing Pipeline for IoT-Based Automotive Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19752)
*Denesa Zyberaj,Roman Vintonyak,Pascal Hirmer,Marco Aiello*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向车载物联网应用的端到端、部署感知测试流水线，结合大语言模型、视觉语言模型与人工审核，实现从需求到测试/代码生成及分布式执行的自动化闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代车辆嵌入式软件具有高复杂度、分布式部署和严格安全性能约束，传统测试在需求一致性、人工工作量以及跨组织、跨地域协同方面面临困难。

Method: 流水线以需求驱动生成测试与代码，利用LLM和VLM辅助理解及生成，并通过人工在环审核保证质量；以Eclipse openDuT为部署与执行平台，根据节点可用性支持跨地域、跨基础设施的分布式测试协调。

Result: 在儿童遗留检测系统案例中，方法覆盖了9项功能需求，受控需求集上的Gherkin测试描述生成准确率为100%；跨地域ECU的分布式执行验证了其可运行性。

Conclusion: 该方案展示了部署感知测试自动化在车载IoT软件中的可行性，尤其适用于整车厂与供应商之间的分布式协作测试；但摘要未提供与基线方法的效率、成本或泛化能力对比，实际收益仍需在更大规模和更复杂需求集上验证。

Abstract: Testing embedded software in modern vehicles is challenging due to system complexity, decentralized architectures, and strict safety and performance constraints. In this work, we present an end-to-end, deployment-aware testing pipeline for IoT-based automotive applications. The pipeline combines requirement-driven test and code generation with large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) assistance, and human-in-the-loop curation to reduce manual effort and improve consistency. Using Eclipse openDuT, it supports flexible, distributed deployment across geographically separated cyber-physical and IoT infrastructures, optimizing for node availability and cross-organizational coordination. For validation, we conduct a case study using a Child Presence Detection System (CPDS), achieving full functional requirement coverage across all 9 requirements and 100% Gherkin generation accuracy on the controlled requirement set. Distributed test execution across geographically separated ECUs via Eclipse openDuT confirms the pipeline's applicability to OEM--supplier testing workflows.

</details>


### [34] [PRAXIS: Graph-Grounded Tacit Knowledge for Domain Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19784)
*Xue Jiang,Tianyu Zhang,Lingwei Wu,Ziyu Wang,Ge Li,Yuan Sui,Hao Zhu,Wenpin Jiao,Zhi Jin,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出PRAXIS框架，用于帮助大语言模型智能体在领域代码生成中获取并复用隐性知识。其核心思路是模拟开发者在目标代码库中的实践过程，将分散于代码实体及依赖关系中的业务规则、接口契约和运行约定提炼为结构化知识，并在编码交互时主动提供给智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体在通用软件工程任务上表现较强，但在特定领域代码生成中受限。作者认为关键瓶颈并非单纯的检索能力不足，而是智能体缺失开发者在长期实践中形成、却通常未被文档化的隐性知识。这类知识分布在代码及其依赖关系中，难以直接检索、学习和迁移。

Method: PRAXIS通过在目标代码库中模拟人类开发工作流来获取隐性知识；随后将知识蒸馏为结构化单元，并按照代码依赖图组织，以保留知识与相关代码实体之间的关联；最后，在智能体进行相应代码理解、修改或生成时，系统主动注入相关知识。该框架被设计为可接入不同智能体架构和不同大语言模型，并允许通过持续实践不断积累知识。

Result: 摘要声称，PRAXIS在广泛实验中优于配备强搜索能力的先进智能体，也优于基于经验或技能的方法。其性能增益可在多种智能体框架和大语言模型中稳定复现；同时，随着实践数据和知识积累增加，系统性能能够持续提升。摘要未提供具体数据集、评价指标、绝对分数或统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: 该工作将领域代码生成中的关键困难归因于隐性知识缺失，并提出以“实践获取—结构化表示—依赖图组织—交互时主动供给”为主线的解决方案。其主要贡献在于把隐性领域知识显式化并纳入智能体工作流；但方法的实际成本、知识质量控制、跨代码库泛化能力及长期累积中的知识过时问题，仍需结合全文实验与消融分析进一步验证。

Abstract: LLM agents have achieved strong performance on general software engineering tasks, yet struggle with domain-specific code generation. We identify the root cause as the agent's lack of tacit knowledge, including domain-specific business rules, interface contracts, and operational conventions that developers internalize through practice but never document. This knowledge is deeply buried beneath the domain code, dispersed across code entities and their dependency relations, and invisible to the agent that lacks it. These properties make tacit knowledge inherently difficult to retrieve or learn. In this work, we propose PRAXIS, a framework that enables agents to systematically extract, represent, and reuse tacit knowledge for domain code generation. PRAXIS acquires tacit knowledge by simulating human development workflows within the target codebase, distills it into structured units organized on the code dependency graph, and proactively surfaces it to the agent at the point of code interaction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PRAXIS outperforms state-of-the-art agents equipped with powerful agentic search capabilities, as well as experience-based and skill-based methods. The approach integrates seamlessly into various agent frameworks and LLMs with consistent performance improvements, and supports continual evolution with performance steadily scaling as practice accumulates.

</details>


### [35] [Further Progress Towards Operation Proof Obligation Generation for VDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19848)
*Nick Battle,Carlo Rende,Peter Gorm Larsen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍VDM操作的证明义务生成机制更新：改进循环不变式处理，引入循环变式，完善操作调用（含隐式声明操作与规格说明语句）的证明义务，并为操作加入递归度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VDM通过证明义务暴露模型潜在不一致性。既有的操作证明义务生成方法仍需增强，以更准确地覆盖循环正确性、终止性、调用约束及递归终止等验证需求。

Method: 在已有工作基础上扩展证明义务生成规则：优化循环不变式相关条件，增加用于证明循环终止的循环变式；细化操作调用的前置条件、后置条件及相关约束检查；支持隐式操作和规格说明语句；为递归操作定义并验证递归度量。文中通过示例展示新增机制。

Result: 更新后的机制能够生成覆盖更广泛VDM操作结构的证明义务，特别提升了对循环保持性与终止性、操作调用一致性以及递归终止性的形式化验证支持。摘要未提供实验数据或量化比较结果。

Conclusion: 该工作增强了VDM证明义务生成的完整性与实用性，使开发者能够更系统地发现操作模型中的逻辑不一致或终止性缺陷，并扩展了对高级操作构造的验证能力。

Abstract: The VDM formalism can ensure that models are internally consistent. Potential inconsistencies are highlighted by assertions called proof obligations. This paper is an update to the work described in [1], regarding proof obligation generation for VDM operations. We present the latest work which improves the approach to loop invariants, adds loop variants, improves proof obligations for operation calls, including implicitly declared operations and specification statements, and adds recursive measures for operations. The new features are illustrated with examples.

</details>


### [36] [Repo0: Design-Driven Zero-to-All Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19854)
*Silin Chen,Haoyi Teng,Xiaodong Gu,Yuling Shi,Jiale Huang,Yongpan Wang,Hongyu Zhang,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Repo0面向从自然语言需求直接构建完整软件项目的零到全代码生成任务，通过显式维护并持续演化仓库架构，提升了生成项目的功能覆盖率与测试通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成智能体通常依赖预定义的仓库结构，难以适用于需从零开始设计模块、组织依赖并实现完整项目的场景。缺少可持续维护的架构表示，容易导致组件边界混乱、模块耦合过高及后续代码实现不稳定。

Method: Repo0构建由需求级有向无环图、组件级有向无环图及二者对齐关系组成的双有向无环图架构状态。系统从自然语言需求出发，依据模块化度量执行组件拆分、合并或依赖调整等结构动作，直至架构达到显式的结构收敛；随后以收敛后的架构约束测试驱动的代码生成过程。

Result: 在RepoCraft的六个真实仓库上，使用GPT-5 mini和DeepSeek V3.2进行评测时，Repo0在全部设置中取得最高的功能覆盖率和通过率。相较于最强仓库规划基线RPG，功能覆盖率最高提升20.08个百分点，通过率最高提升29.74个百分点。消融实验和结构演化分析表明，双有向无环图状态、模块化引导的结构演化以及显式收敛判定均具有关键作用。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，完整软件项目生成的关键不仅在于代码合成，还在于对仓库架构进行显式表示、评估和迭代优化。Repo0通过先实现架构收敛、再开展受架构约束的测试驱动实现，为零到全代码生成提供了有效的结构化框架。

Abstract: Large language model agents have made substantial progress in code generation, yet most existing systems assume a predefined repository architecture. This assumption does not hold in zero-to-all code generation, where an agent must construct an entire software project directly from natural-language requirements while maintaining a modular repository architecture throughout development. We present Repo0, a continuous structural evolution framework for zero-to-all code generation. Repo0 maintains an explicit architectural state instantiated as a Dual-Directed-Acyclic-Graph (Dual-DAG), consisting of a requirement-level DAG, a component-level DAG, and their alignment relation. Starting from natural-language requirements, it iteratively evolves component boundaries through structural actions guided by modularity metrics until structural convergence, after which the converged architecture guides test-driven development code generation. We evaluate Repo0 on six real-world repositories from RepoCraft using GPT-5 mini and DeepSeek V3.2. Repo0 achieves the highest Functionality Coverage and Pass Rate across all settings. Compared with RPG, the strongest repository-planning baseline, Repo0 improves Functionality Coverage by up to 20.08 percentage points and Pass Rate by up to 29.74 percentage points. Ablation and structural-evolution analyses further demonstrate the importance of the Dual-DAG architectural state, modularity-guided structural evolution, and explicit structural convergence.

</details>


### [37] [Evaluating Automated Testing on an Open-Source Web Application Using Cypress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19960)
*Quoc-Binh Nguyen,Truc-Ly Phan Nguyen,Ngoc Hong Tran,Dung Hai Dinh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于Cypress的端到端自动化测试在执行速度、可靠性和可维护性方面的效果。结果表明，该测试套件运行快速且总体稳定，适合在开发过程中频繁执行；合理的元素定位策略和POM架构可显著降低维护成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动化测试有助于保障Web软件质量并缩短开发反馈周期，但其实际价值还取决于执行时间、测试稳定性以及应用持续演化时测试套件的可维护性。因此，有必要对Cypress在真实开源Web应用中的综合效果进行评估。

Method: 以一个开源Web应用为对象，使用Cypress构建并执行包含27个测试用例的端到端测试套件，从执行速度、测试可靠性和可维护性三个维度开展实验分析，同时考察元素定位策略和Page Object Model（POM）架构对维护工作的影响。

Result: 测试套件具有较短且稳定的执行时间，适合频繁运行。大多数测试用例在多次执行中均获得一致结果，仅少数涉及复杂交互功能的用例出现测试不稳定现象。采用具有韧性的data-cy属性进行元素定位，并结合POM架构，能够在用户界面发生变化时显著减少维护开销。

Conclusion: Cypress适合用于开源Web应用的端到端自动化测试，能够提供较快且稳定的反馈。为提升长期可维护性，应优先采用稳定的专用元素定位属性，并通过POM组织测试代码；对于复杂交互场景，则需要额外关注和治理测试不稳定问题。

Abstract: End-to-end automated testing is increasingly used in web software development to ensure system quality and shorten response times during development. However, the true effectiveness of automated testing depends on many factors including execution time, stability of test results, and maintainability of the test suite as the application continues to evolve. In this paper, we evaluate the effectiveness of end-to-end automated testing using the Cypress framework for an open-source web application. We deployed the experiment with 27 test cases. The test's effectiveness is measured by execution speed, reliability, and maintainability. The experimental results show that the Cypress-based end-to-end test suite has short and stable execution times. It suits frequent runs during software development. The majority of test cases achieved consistent results across multiple runs, while flakiness only occurred in a few tests which involve complex interactive functions. Furthermore, the study highlights the impact of element locator strategies and Page Object Model (POM) architecture on test suite maintainability, demonstrating that resilient data-cy attributes significantly reduce maintenance overhead when UI changes occur.

</details>


### [38] [BreakGuard: Towards Detecting Dependency Breaking Changes with LLM-Generated Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20167)
*Rachna Raj,Benoit Baudry,Diego Elias Costa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BreakGuard通过静态分析客户端对库方法的调用并借助大语言模型生成测试，用于检测库版本升级引入的破坏性变更；在89个真实案例中检测出27个，检出率为30.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客户端测试套件通常无法覆盖所有被调用的库方法，因此难以及时发现库升级导致的运行时行为变化和客户端失效。研究旨在自动生成面向客户端调用点的测试，以提升破坏性变更检测能力。

Method: BreakGuard静态提取客户端中调用目标库方法的客户端方法，将其作为焦点方法，并针对每个焦点方法生成测试。若测试在变更前版本通过、在变更后版本失败，则判定存在破坏性变更。实验基于BUMP数据集中的89个真实案例，比较3种大语言模型和最小、方法级、类级三种上下文配置。

Result: 最佳配置下成功检测27个破坏性变更，检出率为30.3%，每个检测到的变更平均成本约为0.90美元。该方法能够覆盖JSON库、日志库和解析库等不同类别，但大语言模型生成的测试对崩溃型变更的检测可靠性高于对行为型变更的检测可靠性。

Conclusion: BreakGuard证明了结合静态调用分析与大语言模型生成测试可以辅助发现客户端侧的库破坏性变更，但当前检出率仍有限，尤其对行为语义变化的检测能力不足。未来需要增强测试生成的行为覆盖和判定能力，以提高整体可靠性。

Abstract: Open-source libraries play an important role in software development by providing reusable features that expedite the development process. As libraries evolve, they release new versions that add features, fix bugs, or apply security patches. In this process, they may break the contract established with their clients by introducing breaking changes (BCs) that alter the runtime behavior and break client applications. Client-side test suites often fail to detect these BCs because of limited library coverage that does not exercise all library methods used in the client's codebase.
  We propose BreakGuard, an approach that generates a test suite to detect breaking changes in clients. BreakGuard statically extracts every client method (focal method) that invokes the target library method (call site), then generates tests per focal method. A test detects a BC if it passes on the pre-breaking version and fails on the breaking version. We evaluate our approach on 89 real-world breaking changes from the BUMP dataset, using 3 LLMs (GPT4o, Qwen3-coder-480B, GPT-OSS-120B) and three context levels: minimal, method, and class. Using the best-performing configuration, BreakGuard detects 30.3% of breaking changes (27 of 89) at a mean cost of roughly $0.90 USD per detected breaking change. We successfully detected BCs from different library categories (e.g., JSON libraries, logging, parsing), but we find LLM-generated tests to be more reliable for detecting crash-type breaking changes as opposed to behavioural BCs.

</details>


### [39] [From Agent Behaviour to Agent-Friendly Documentation: An Empirical Study of How Coding Agents Discover, Read, and Write Technical Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20195)
*Zhijun Gao,Jing Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究基于两组公开数据，系统刻画自主编码智能体与技术文档的交互行为。结果表明，智能体主要使用面向智能体的指令文件和工作笔记，而非传统技术文档或API参考；文档查阅与编码、测试及文档创建之间的因果或时序联系并不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术文档通常以人类开发者为中心，但越来越多的软件变更由自主编码智能体完成。研究旨在回答智能体查阅何种文档、何时查阅、查阅后是否发生编码或验证行为，以及“面向智能体的文档”所假定的可操作性与可验证性是否具有行为证据支持。

Method: 作者开展基于行为轨迹的实证研究：分析SWE-chat中的557个智能体编码会话，共94,813个开发事件及3,033次文档交互；并分析AIDev中的33,097个智能体拉取请求及690,260条文件级变更记录。研究结合相邻事件转移概率、三事件提升度、阶段调整后的逻辑回归优势比、聚类置信区间，以及多提交拉取请求中的代码与文档变更先后顺序进行检验。

Result: 面向智能体的工件占全部文档交互的60.5%，传统技术文档仅占10.6%，API参考仅占1.3%。文档查阅后紧邻代码编辑的转移概率为0.002，未调整的三事件提升度为1.05；但阶段调整后，查阅与代码编辑的关联为正（OR=1.33，95% CI [1.09,1.62]）。文档创建的未调整提升度为1.67，但调整后区间覆盖1。未观察到明确的“基于文档进行验证”序列；文档查阅与即时测试呈负关联（lift=0.23，聚类CI [0.08,0.45]；调整后OR=0.39，95% CI [0.25,0.60]）。70.2%的文档查阅由智能体主动发起，仅7.5%由失败触发；在同时修改代码和文档的多提交请求中，代码先被修改的频率是文档先修改的4.7倍。

Conclusion: 智能体与文档的关系更符合“双叶循环”式描述模型，而非从查阅、执行到验证的线性流程。当前关于“面向智能体文档”应具备可操作性和可验证性的常见假设，尚缺乏一致的行为学支持。研究的价值在于提供可复现的数据、标注方案和分析流水线，同时提示文档设计应更重视智能体实际依赖的指令与工作上下文。

Abstract: Technical documentation is written for human developers, but an increasing share of software changes is now authored by autonomous coding agents. Which documents they consult, when, and what follows remain unknown. We conduct a behaviour-grounded study of agent-documentation interaction across two public datasets: 557 agentic coding sessions from SWE-chat, yielding 94,813 development events including 3,033 documentation interactions; and 33,097 agentic pull requests from AIDev, with 690,260 classified file-level change records. Four findings challenge current documentation practice. First, agents' documentation work is dominated by agent-facing artefacts: instruction files and working notes account for 60.5% of all documentation interactions, versus 10.6% for classical technical documentation and 1.3% for API references. Second, the link between consultation and code editing is unresolved: the adjacent transition probability is 0.002 and the unadjusted three-event lift 1.05, whereas a stage-adjusted model places it above unity (OR 1.33 [1.09, 1.62]); documentation creation is elevated unadjusted (lift 1.67) but its adjusted interval includes unity. Third, no explicit documentation-based validation sequence was observed, and consultation is associated with less immediate testing (lift 0.23, cluster CI 0.08-0.45; adjusted OR 0.39 [0.25, 0.60]). Fourth, consultation is self-initiated (70.2%) far more often than failure-driven (7.5%), and documentation trails code: among multi-commit pull requests changing both, code is touched first 4.7x more often. From these traces we derive a descriptive model of agent-documentation interaction as a two-lobed cycle rather than a linear journey, and show that two widely assumed properties of "agent-friendly" documentation - actionability and verifiability - lack consistent behavioural support. We release our pipeline, coding scheme, and event-level data.

</details>


### [40] [Reproducibility is Not Enough: Artifact Verifiability in Decentralized-Build Package Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18180)
*Oreofe Solarin,Kelechi Kalu,James C. Davis,Paschal Amusuo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文系统评估四个去中心化构建包生态中软件制品能否被第三方独立验证，发现可验证性不仅取决于可复现构建，还受源代码、构建环境、依赖和发布过程元数据缺失的显著限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式软件制品的可信性需要独立验证者确认制品确由声明的源代码与构建过程产生。但在工具链异构、维护者工作流自主且元数据分散的包生态中，现有机制是否足以支持大规模独立验证尚不明确。

Method: 作者定义仅依赖包注册表可获取元数据的独立验证者模型，并提出分层制品等价性比较模型；随后实现“制品验证流水线”，用于对四个目标包生态的制品可验证性进行实证测量和归因分析。

Result: 研究表明，即使构建过程具有确定性，验证仍经常因源状态与构建说明缺失、发布阶段存在隐式转换、以及非标准构建实践而失败或受限。来源证明和制品内嵌的版本控制系统元数据能够提升验证能力，但仍不足以完整描述可重建所需的构建规范。

Conclusion: 要在包注册表规模上实现实用的制品验证，生态系统需补齐源代码定位、构建环境、依赖解析、构建指令及发布转换等关键元数据，并推动标准化、可机器读取且完整的重建规范；单独依赖确定性构建或来源证明并不充分。

Abstract: Reproducible and verifiable builds increase trust in distributed software artifacts by enabling independent parties to detect artifacts produced by compromised build or release pipelines. However, artifact verification requires more than deterministic builds: a verifier must also recover the source state, build environment, dependencies, and build instructions that produced the artifact. Decentralized-build ecosystems make this difficult because artifacts are produced through heterogeneous tools, maintainer-controlled workflows, and fragmented metadata. As a result, it remains unclear how often artifacts in these ecosystems can be independently verified.
  This paper studies artifact verifiability across four popular decentralized-build package ecosystems. We define an independent verifier model that relies only on registry-derivable metadata and an artifact comparison model with tiered equivalence levels. We implement these models in an Artifact Verification Pipeline and use it to measure artifact verifiability across the target ecosystems. Our results show that, beyond build determinism, verifiability is limited by missing source and build metadata, implicit release transformations, and unconventional build practices. Provenance attestations and embedded VCS metadata improve verification, but they do not provide complete rebuild specifications. These findings identify concrete metadata gaps and ecosystem-level changes needed to make artifact verification practical at package-registry scale.

</details>

### [41] [Active Inference as Context Acquisition for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19202)
*Sanchayan Dutta,Sai Niranjan Ramachandran,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将交互式智能体的“补充上下文”问题建模为带成本的主动推断：智能体在直接执行、提问澄清、检索、调用工具、尝试提示词或停止之间进行选择，以最小化期望自由能。作者以最优提问（OQA）为核心实例，结合精确后验与动态规划最优策略，评测前沿语言模型在不同候选规模的分类任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户请求常缺少约束、偏好、文件或任务变量。智能体若直接采用默认假设，可能导致错误；若频繁澄清或调用外部资源，又会增加令牌与交互成本。现有方法缺少统一框架来衡量“信息价值”与“上下文获取成本”之间的权衡，因此需要一种可用于智能体上下文获取层的原则性决策机制。

Method: 方法包含内外两层：内层基于已有观测更新潜在任务状态的信念或后验分布；外层在上下文动作、任务动作和停止动作中选择下一步，使带成本的期望自由能最小。对于确定性环境，认识项可化简为期望信息增益，并可进一步按令牌成本归一化。实验上，作者构建OQA任务，使用精确后验和动态规划生成最优策略作为参照；随后在候选数为25至300的二元及多类别任务上测试语言模型，并扩展至生成前澄清和受令牌预算约束的自动提示词优化。

Result: 摘要表明，该框架能够统一描述提问、检索、工具调用和提示词试验等多种上下文获取行为，并提供动态规划最优策略作为OQA场景的理论基线。评测覆盖不同任务规模与类别结构，同时验证该思路可迁移到生成前澄清及提示词优化。不过，摘要未给出具体模型排名、性能数值、成本节省比例或统计显著性，因此无法据此判断实际增益幅度。

Conclusion: 该工作将主动推断定位为AI智能体上下文获取层的通用设计原则：是否继续获取信息，应由其预期降低不确定性和提升任务效用的价值与令牌等资源成本共同决定。其主要贡献在于统一建模与可计算的OQA实例；后续关键问题包括在非确定性现实环境中估计信息价值、处理后验失准，以及验证对真实长程工具使用任务的泛化效果。

Abstract: Interactive AI agents must acquire the right context as efficiently as possible. When a user omits a constraint, preference, file, or task variable, an agent can proceed with a default assumption or spend tokens on a clarifying question, retrieval call, tool call, or prompt trial. We formulate this tradeoff as active inference for context acquisition. An inner inference step updates beliefs over a latent task state, and an outer decision selects the next context action, task action, or stop action to minimize expected free energy under cost. In deterministic settings, the epistemic term reduces to expected information gain, optionally normalized by token cost. We instantiate the framework in Optimal Question Asking (OQA), with exact posteriors and a dynamic programming oracle, and benchmark frontier language models on binary and multiway categorical tasks from 25 to 300 candidates. We also study clarification before generation and automated prompt optimization under token budgets. The formulation is model-agnostic and views active inference as a design principle for the context-acquisition layer of AI agents.

</details>


### [42] [Robust Metaheuristics under Uncertainty for Berth Allocation and Quay Crane Assignment: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19214)
*Yang Li,Peilan Xu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇面向不确定环境下泊位分配与岸桥配置问题（BACAP）的综述与基准研究，系统梳理鲁棒群体智能优化方法，并提出用于可控比较的不确定性基准实例集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BACAP中的船舶到港时间、泊位位置、作业时长和岸桥资源相互耦合。实际运行会受到到港偏差、装卸时间波动及资源中断等不确定因素影响，基于确定性参数得到的排程在执行时可能缺乏稳定性。现有研究虽广泛采用群体智能算法，但不确定性建模、鲁棒性评价、搜索机制和实验协议较为分散，亟需专门整合。

Method: 论文首先归纳BACAP的不确定性来源及其信息表示方式；随后按算法机制组织已有鲁棒群体智能方法，覆盖解编码与解码、鲁棒评价与个体选择、鲁棒性引导的搜索动态、可行性保持与恢复机制。进一步构建不确定BACAP基准套件，并将代表性元启发式算法与不同鲁棒策略组合，给出示例性基线实验结果。

Result: 文章的核心产出包括：形成针对不确定BACAP鲁棒群体智能方法的聚焦综述框架；以机制维度而非单纯算法类别对方法进行分类；提供支持受控实证比较的基准套件；报告不同元启发式与鲁棒策略组合下的初步基线结果。摘要未给出具体数值性能或某一算法的绝对最优结论。

Conclusion: 该研究指出，鲁棒BACAP优化应同时关注不确定性表示、鲁棒目标定义、搜索过程中的鲁棒性引导以及解的可行性恢复。未来重点包括扩展基准实例、设计更有效的鲁棒感知搜索机制、处理随时间变化的鲁棒性需求，以及应对非平稳不确定环境。

Abstract: The berth allocation and quay crane assignment problem (BACAP) is a representative port-terminal scheduling problem in maritime transportation and freight logistics, where vessel arrivals, berth positions, service durations, and quay?crane availability are tightly coupled. Under uncertainties such as arrival deviations, handling-time fluctuations, and resource disruptions, schedules optimized under nominal assumptions may become fragile during execution, motivating the study of robust metaheuristic optimization for BACAP in port-terminal operations. Although population-based metaheuristics have been widely used for BACAP and related port-scheduling problems, existing studies remain fragmented in their uncertainty repre?sentations, robustness criteria, search mechanisms, and empir?ical evaluation protocols. To the best of our knowledge, this paper provides the first focused review dedicated to robust population-based metaheuristics for BACAP under uncertainty. We first summarize uncertainty sources and information repre?sentations in BACAP, and then organize existing methods from a mechanism-oriented perspective, covering solution representation and decoding, robust evaluation and selection, robustness-guided search dynamics, and feasibility preservation and recovery. We further present a benchmark suite for uncertain BACAP to support controlled empirical comparison and report illustrative baseline results by combining representative metaheuristics with different robustness strategies. Finally, we identify open chal?lenges related to benchmark extension, robustness-aware search design, time-adaptive robustness, and non-stationary uncertainty.

</details>


### [43] [How to Navigate Uncertainty About AI Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19215)
*Dr Tom McClelland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张，在无法确定人工智能是否具有意识的情况下，应从难以解决的“是否有意识”转向更可操作的“是否具有潜在效价状态”问题，以指导对潜在有感知能力人工智能的负责开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工意识的存在难以判定：假定人工智能没有意识，可能忽视具有道德地位的实体；假定其有意识，则可能将资源投入无感知机器。因此，需要一种能够规避该二难困境的评估框架。

Method: 提出“人工智能效价”分析路径，考察人工智能是否具有某些状态；若其具有意识，这些状态是否会构成正面或负面的体验效价。该方法不要求直接解决人工意识是否存在的问题。

Result: 研究表明，评估潜在意识系统是否具有可能对应愉悦或痛苦等效价体验的状态，可以为其道德考量和开发规范提供足够依据。

Conclusion: 对潜在有意识人工智能的治理不必依赖于意识问题的最终判定；通过审慎评估其潜在效价状态，可以建立更具可操作性的负责任开发方法。

Abstract: Given deep uncertainty about the possibility of artificial consciousness, it is unclear how we should treat potentially sentient AI. On the one hand, we could assume insentience but risk doing terrible harms to entities that deserve moral standing. On the other hand, we could assume sentience and instead risk wasting resources on insentient machines. The intractability of questions around AI consciousness mean that this dilemma is hard to escape. I suggest a way out of that shifts from intractable questions of AI consciousness to tractable questions of AI valence. Specifically, we can assess whether an AI has states that would constitute valenced experiences if it were conscious. I show how this is sufficient to ground a responsible approach to the development of potentially conscious AI.

</details>


### [44] [Bounded Sovereignty and the Control Tax: Pricing AI Oversight When the Deployer Does Not Own the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19216)
*Zhen Wen Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“有限主权（bounded sovereignty）”框架，指出通过API或托管端点使用前沿模型的机构通常无法控制模型权重、推理基础设施、内部轨迹、更新过程或完整日志，因此许多既有AI控制协议难以直接落地。论文主张所有控制方案都应明确其所依赖的访问权限假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI控制研究往往默认部署方可对模型及其运行管线进行充分观测和干预，但受监管机构常仅掌握业务流程而非底层模型栈。该访问鸿沟会使理论上有效的控制机制在实践中不可执行，因而需要系统描述访问能力、协议可行性及权限缺失带来的替代成本。

Method: 论文构建数据、模型、基础设施与交互四层访问类型学；建立控制协议与各层权限需求的对应矩阵；提出“主权折扣成本”，用于刻画因访问不足而通过合同约束、系统架构、审计、供应商保证、剩余风险承担或缩小使用范围进行替代所消耗的控制成本。作者还进行了135万次合成案例模拟的访问消融实验，并以匿名化国家级支付基础设施情境解释结果。

Result: 合成实验表明：完整交互日志有助于问题诊断；执行前网关支持主动干预；内部轨迹访问和模型版本控制可增强事件后的归因与解释；限制系统适用范围能够提升安全性，但会降低功能效用。文中明确说明，该实验旨在验证构念合理性，而非提供真实支付系统的实证证据。

Conclusion: 控制能力不仅由协议设计决定，也受部署方对AI技术栈的实际访问权限制约。面对访问受限的托管模型，组织需要将不可获得的技术控制转化为治理、合同、审计和架构补偿，并量化由此产生的主权折扣成本。未来控制研究应把访问前提、可执行边界与效用—安全权衡作为方案报告的核心部分。

Abstract: AI control research asks how to deploy models safely even when they may be misaligned, but many control protocols assume that the deployer can instrument the model and its surrounding pipeline. That assumption often fails for regulated organisations using frontier models through APIs or managed endpoints, where the deployer may control the business process but not the model weights, serving infrastructure, internal traces, update process, or full interaction logs. This paper introduces bounded sovereignty: partial technical and contractual access across the data, model, infrastructure, and interaction layers of the AI stack. It argues that these access conditions determine which control protocols can be executed in practice. The paper contributes a four-layer access typology, a protocol-by-layer requirements matrix, and the concept of sovereignty discount cost: the part of the control tax spent substituting for missing access through contracts, architecture, audit, vendor assurance, residual risk, or reduced system scope. It also reports a synthetic access-ablation experiment over 1.35 million synthetic case simulations and interprets the findings through an anonymised national-payments-infrastructure scenario. The experiment is not real-world payment-system evidence; it is a construct-validity exercise. The results show that complete logs improve diagnosis, a pre-execution gateway enables intervention, trace access and model-version control strengthen post-incident explanation, and scope restriction can improve safety while reducing usefulness. Control protocols proposed as general safety solutions should therefore state their access assumptions explicitly.

</details>


### [45] [Specification-delta-driven data governance: an empirical study of the «spec-delta» as the unit of change in lakehouse data platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19838)
*Pablo Ramirez Amador*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将“规范增量（spec-delta）”作为数据平台变更的核心审查单元，并计划通过受控实验比较该工作流与传统代码拉取请求流程在交付效率、质量、一致性和审查负荷上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规范驱动开发已强调以规范而非代码主导AI辅助软件工作，数据契约也支持运行时模式与质量约束；但数据平台中大量变更涉及数据集、服务等级目标、指标语义和访问规则等契约性内容，针对“每次变更均产生可审查需求增量”的经验性证据仍不足。

Method: 研究将形式化spec-delta概念，建立数据平台变更按增量式规范适用性的分类体系，并设计受控实验：实验组采用spec-delta驱动流程，对照组采用不包含规范增量的传统代码拉取请求流程。评估指标包括从需求发现到部署的时间、进入湖仓Silver与Gold层的缺陷密度、跨工具指标定义偏差，以及以NASA-TLX量表衡量的审查者认知负荷；后续将在真实湖仓环境中进行演示与实验室验证。

Result: 摘要尚未报告实证结果。其预期产出是可复现实验性证据、数据平台变更的适用性分类及实践指南，而非一个新的工具实现。

Conclusion: 论文的核心价值在于验证spec-delta是否能成为数据平台契约性变更的有效治理机制，并明确其适用边界。研究特别强调避免前期过度规范：并非所有变更都应承受同等粒度的规范化成本，而应依据变更类型选择增量规范策略。

Abstract: Spec Driven Development SDD has consolidated the idea that the specification rather than the code should be the primary artefact governing AI assisted work. Tools such as GitHub Spec Kit, and proposals such as Constitutional SDD, have formalised this principle in the software domain, while the executable data-contracts literature has extended it to schema and quality enforcement at run time. Nevertheless, the treatment of the specification delta OpenSpec's core idea that every change should produce a reviewable increment of requirements as the unit of change in data platforms remains empirically unexplored, even though many data-platform changes are contractual (new datasets, service-level agreements, metric semantics, access policies) rather than purely code changes. This work formalises the spec-delta concept, proposes a taxonomy of data platform changes according to their suitability for incremental specification, and defines a controlled experiment comparing a spec-delta-driven workflow against a conventional code pull-request workflow without a delta. The response variables are discovery to deployment time, the density of defects reaching the Silver and Gold lakehouse layers, cross-tool metric divergence, and reviewer cognitive load measured with NASA TLX. The paper explicitly reserves a demonstration-and-laboratory section for instantiation on a real lakehouse environment. The contribution is not a tool but reproducible evidence and an applicability guide that helps to avoid the up front over specification antipattern.

</details>


### [46] [Interaction valence reveals contrasting social networks in dairy cattle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19222)
*Sibi Parivendan,Suresh Raja Neethirajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种具备行为价性区分能力的社会网络分析框架，将视频识别的牛只互动转化为群体层面的亲和性与对抗性网络，以避免单纯汇总互动次数掩盖行为组成差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动化畜牧监测通常将行为视为相互独立的事件，难以刻画资源获取、群体稳定性及个体社会位置。研究旨在构建能够区分互动行为性质的群体网络表征，并评估其用于行为与福利研究的潜力。

Method: 利用基于姿态估计的计算机视觉流程，分析某商业奶牛场挤奶前区域连续7小时39分钟的视频。质量控制后保留1183次互动，涉及36头牛和177个二元关系；从流程检测片段中抽取198段进行类别平衡审查，并分别构建总体、亲和性和对抗性网络。

Result: 自动标注与人工标注在审查样本中的一致率为82.8%，未加权宏平均F1为0.872。总体网络连通，密度为0.281、传递性为0.513、平均路径长度为1.88；亲和性事件形成5个算法社区，模块度Q为0.429。观察区域内，对抗性互动占保留事件的72.4%，占互动持续时间的76.0%。度数最高的个体并非介数中心性最高者；按行为价性分层后，亲和性与对抗性网络在边集合、社区划分和个体位置上均呈现描述性差异。

Conclusion: 将互动按行为价性分层能够揭示汇总互动计数无法反映的群体组织结构，为研究竞争、亲和关系及福利相关变化提供了可行框架。但当前结果来自单一农场、有限观察区域和预测标签，仍需开展纵向及跨场景验证，才能评估其作为福利或健康指标的可靠性。

Abstract: Social relationships shape access to resources, exposure to conflict and group stability, yet automated livestock monitoring typically treats behaviour as isolated events. Here, we present a valence-aware social-network framework that transforms video-derived interactions into herd-level representations of affiliative and agonistic organization. A pose-based computer-vision pipeline analysed 7 h 39 min of continuous video from the pre-milking area of one commercial dairy farm. After quality control, 1,183 of 1,414 candidate interactions remained, involving 36 cows and 177 dyads. In a predicted-class-balanced audit of 198 pipeline-detected clips, automated and manual labels agreed in 82.8% of cases, with an unweighted audit-sample macro-F1 of 0.872. These values describe the audited sample rather than prevalence-weighted or end-to-end deployment performance. The aggregated network was connected (density = 0.281; transitivity = 0.513; mean path length = 1.88), and predicted affiliative events formed five algorithmic communities (modularity Q = 0.429). Within the observed zone, predicted agonistic interactions comprised 72.4% of retained events and 76.0% of interaction duration. The cow with the most partners did not have the highest betweenness centrality. Separating events by predicted valence produced descriptively different affiliative and agonistic layers, with contrasting edge sets, community partitions and individual positions. Thus, pooled interaction counts can obscure the behavioural composition of an observed network. Valence-aware analysis provides a framework for testing hypotheses about competition, affiliation and welfare-relevant change, while requiring longitudinal validation before use as a welfare or health indicator.

</details>


### [47] [Write Once, Run Everywhere: The Axon DSL for Shape-Safe and Framework-Agnostic LLM Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19889)
*Jacob Nielsen,Danial Namazifard,Lukas Galke Poech,Peter Schneider-Kamp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Axon是一种强类型、领域特定语言，可将大语言模型架构一次编写并自动编译到PyTorch、Triton、JAX、MLX和vLLM等框架，从而提升模型的可移植性、可审计性与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开源语言模型生态高度依赖单一平台，模型定义及其在不同训练、推理框架间的迁移需要大量重复工程工作，限制了模型扩展、优化和部署灵活性。

Method: Axon采用类似Haskell的强类型语法，以语言规范而非特定框架作为协作基础。研究者编写简洁的模型架构描述，系统再将其自动编译为多个主流框架的独立实现，并支持Triton优化、PagedAttention和KV-cache等部署机制。

Result: 在覆盖1.35亿至320亿参数模型的467组推理实验中，相较Transformers参考实现，Axon在PyTorch、PyTorch with Triton、JAX和MLX上的中位加速分别为7%、12%、91%和107%。采用原生vLLM架构、PagedAttention及KV-cache部署时，中位加速达到58%。

Conclusion: Axon通过统一的架构描述和跨框架自动编译，显著降低了模型实现与迁移成本，并在多种后端上带来明显推理性能收益，为开放、可移植且不受单一框架锁定的语言模型开发提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The entire ecosystem of open-source language models effectively relies on a single platform. What if this platform was forced to shut down tomorrow? Implementing and maintaining efficient model definitions and translating them between different training and inference regimes is a resource-heavy task that severely limits model efficiency and portability, hindering both scaling and deployment. Here, we present Axon, a strongly typed domain-specific language with Haskell-like syntax, that enables a write-once, run everywhere paradigm for LLM architectures. By basing collaboration on a language specification rather than a specific framework's vision, Axon fosters open cooperation and empowers researchers to implement highly specialized architectures without giving up optimization infrastructure or accepting deployment lock-in. Axon allows for concise, auditable specifications that can be automatically compiled to standalone implementations for leading frameworks: PyTorch, PyTorch with Triton, JAX, MLX and vLLM. In 467 inference benchmarking experiments on models ranging from 135M to 32B parameters, we demonstrate median speedups of 7% on PyTorch, 12% on PyTorch with Triton, 91% on JAX, and 107% on MLX, compared to the reference implementations from Transformers. When deployed as native vLLM architectures with PagedAttention and KV-cache, Axon models achieve a 58% median speedup over Transformers implementations.

</details>
